# Universe24: the one run

This document stands on the branch engine-5.0-test, the engine 5.0 under test (the Boss's word of the owner, 2026-10-25, 16:3x UTC): main is 4.0, the tree of 0fcc0403d (2026-10-24, 10:12 UTC, the families and the parameters declared, where Universe24 ran), returned by the owner's word of 2026-10-25 (15:2x UTC); main's documents are 4.0's and are not edited; the four documents are kept on the branch alone, where the generic Node's program continues under test, nothing on it a release. This document is the run's: what Universe24 is, what its one run must show, the gates the owner approved, the record's verdict on each gate by name, what is open, and the commands that run and read the record. It is one of the project's four documents (the owner's word of 2026-10-25, 10:0x UTC): [docs/HIGHLIGHTS.md](HIGHLIGHTS.md) holds the postulates and the decisions in force, [docs/ALGEBRA.md](ALGEBRA.md) the law, [docs/ENGINE.md](ENGINE.md) the engine, and this one the run. Where a line here points at the law, the law's line governs and no formula is restated. Every number of the record below is read from the record's logs (`examples/events/universe24/universe24.clicks.jsonl`, the record; `universe24.eras.jsonl` and `universe24.nodes.jsonl`, the board's looks beside it) through the one decoder's tables (`readings_clicks.json`) and the folder's `notes.md`; none is typed from memory. The record read is that of main c60804dc7 (PUSH 37, main 7366816f7; 2,400 intervals of 2026-10-24; the byte check by a second run equal through 1,561, the whole second run waived by the owner's word of 2026-10-25, 00:1x UTC). What does not match nature is named, never hidden; nothing is left open without a name.

## 1. What Universe24 is

One world, one universe, one Node (the project's postulates in one place, the head of docs/ALGEBRA.md, The postulates, since 2026-10-26). The world file is the universe, the board's shape (a cubic lattice wrapped on every axis, a torus), the run's length and, since the owner's approval of 2026-10-25 (ALGEBRA.md, **The seed is a list of lays**), the seed as a list of lays in three forms, `{"universe": ..., "shape": [5, 5, 5], "intervals": 2400, "seed": [...]}`; the record's file of this document holds the first three ("three things and nothing else" to that approval, kept by name; the engine's loader refuses the key `seed` on this branch, the one src line the item changes). The universe file of the engine's record is three integers and nothing else, `{"den", "node_clock", "quantum_action"}`: den, Gamma the Node clock and T the whole piece's action, nature's constants alone (the law since 2026-10-25 adds the lines' pairs as numbers, no number in the code, and derives G at the load from Gamma, K-S1 of 08:2x UTC GitHub's clock, the key `link_unit` of 06:4x to 08:2x kept by name; the conditions on Gamma, T and den the loader's refusals, **The constants of the universe**, ALGEBRA.md, **The Node**; the engine reads the three, ENGINE.md, section 3); a file naming any other key is refused at load (HIGHLIGHTS.md, the postulates 1, 6 and 8; ALGEBRA.md, **The Node**; **No family is declared: the families emerge at the click**). The law's line of 2026-10-25 (ALGEBRA.md, **The universe holds the lines' pairs as numbers**) puts the lines' pairs [num, den] in the universe file beside the three as constants read once at the load, numbers and no names; the engine of this branch and the record below read the three integers and derive den's circle at the load (ENGINE.md, sections 2 and 3). The standing rule of the method (the owner's word of that day): the former way is forbidden, families declared by name, pairs fitted to the board or to nature's ratios, holders and reads typed in; what the engine shows is derived, in order, two blind hands, the cold read, the owner's word on the sentence, the code from the agreed derivation, each property with its small test. The loader lays the seeds (light at one quantum on every Node of the torus but the origin, the hole) and reads the three integers and the shape; after that everything happens by itself at the Nodes: every Node does Rule3 and the same fixed acts from what it reads over one Link, and writes a line, a click, where it reads a whole quantum (ALGEBRA.md, **The Node**; **The board reads itself**; **The bound body's click is the Node's act**). No body, no detector, no family, no window and no rate is declared; the births, the bodies, the families, the masses, the clock shifts, the bangs and the horizon are the decoder's readings of the clicks (postulates 7 and 9). What is tested is emergence (the owner's word of 2026-10-25, 18:1x UTC): the loader lays one seed, the universe file of numbers, the board, the sea of light and the first bodies, and from that seed alone everything runs, the wells, the Links' lengthening and shortening, the turns, the bangs, the births on the Link, the bodies, the clicks; nothing is put in by hand after the load; that is the one trial of Universe24 and the nineteen gates are its reading.

The one run is the project's only main run (HIGHLIGHTS.md, The runs and the record): Universe24's run of its declared intervals through `tools/eras.py` with its three logs, re-recorded whenever the engine changes under it, its pages drawn from the logs alone and its numbers in the folder's notes and in this document, never as a test's pin. The former worlds (the two slits, the big bang and the cycle, the atom, the clicks worlds, Bell and GHZ) stand as frozen records and run nothing (README.md, The former courses, by name).

## 2. What the run must show: the gates

The owner's words of 2026-10-25 (08:0x to 09:0x UTC, through the Boss; HIGHLIGHTS.md, the decision The record's gates, approved 08:5x UTC): general relativity and the physics of reality must show in the clicks at the decoder; the record's gates are the nine phenomena, general relativity by name and the physics of reality, each a reading of the run's logs through the decoder with no declaration in the loader, each SHOWN or NOT SHOWN by name on every record. The gates' predicates and the record's verdicts below are the agreed derivation of two blind hands (G1 and G2, compared cold by the Boss, 2026-10-25, #2187 comment 6392619280), every number their reading of the record's three logs on c60804dc7; the predicates are an existence, a sign, an order or a monotonicity, never a typed number. "Board's" marks a gate that reads the whole board (the eras or the nodes log) and no inside observer; the others read the clicks (the proper, the position, the readings). The gates stand in code on this branch: `tools/record_gates.py` (`PYTHONPATH=src python tools/record_gates.py examples/events/universe24`, the folder positional), one function per gate, `gate_<name>(record)`, returning SHOWN or NOT SHOWN with its numbers, the board's view named, no typed threshold; and tests/test_the_record_gates.py asserting the SHOWN set and the NOT SHOWN set by name on the committed logs. Run on the record of c60804dc7 the tool reads 13 of 23 verdicts SHOWN, the four gates with two verdicts counted twice (light's presence and its redshift; the horizon's Links and silence and its stopped clock; the conservation's identity and its exactness; the mass ratios' fit and their reading from the clicks), the same verdicts as the table below. The nineteen are the list the toy's record is read against, filled by tools/record_gates.py's verdicts and never by hand, no gate dropped and none added without the owner's word; the fit to nature is as close as the integers allow (the mass ratios to 10^-7 at the 54-bit den) and never nature's precision, said plainly here. A verdict of this document changes only on the Boss's request, from a table or a log. Since 11:0x UTC (the owner's word of 2026-10-25, 11:0x UTC, through the Boss, #2187 comment 6396810268, verbatim in translation: "everything you wrote that did not come out must enter the 19 gates; everything is to be seen there; so the order of the checks is one: you check on a Node, you check on the toy, and then it is to be seen in Universe24 too, so that in Universe24 there is no surprise that things do not happen"): everything not shown enters the gates, every item named NOT SHOWN or a finding against nature by name in the comparisons a gate of the table with its predicate and no note (the exact take, the bodies from born pairs, the collision, free fall, the horizon's stopped clock, the redshift of light, Coulomb's sign, the momentum across the click, the merger, the spinor's two beats and the split, the exclusion, the black body, the exponential decay, light on light, the x polarisation, the Casimir force, the running alpha, the neutron's three-child list, the neutrino, and every other such line; the gates of the phenomena, the table below, 20 to 52 by the numbering of that day and the Reader's rows since), and the order of the checks is one: the Node, then the toy, then Universe24; every gate has its one-Node test where one Node can show it, then its reading on the toy's record (the chiral emergence seed and the bodies' seed, over the cycles), and only then its reading on Universe24's record, which confirms what the toy showed and names what it did not; David's decoder reads the same gates(run) on both records, and the Boss runs both. A derived gate is a prediction, and the record must show it (the owner's word of 2026-10-25, 11:0x UTC, through the Boss, #2187 comment 6396841814, verbatim in translation: "everything that was derived and is in the rest must happen when you check; no, there are things that are not on a Node, but those things have to be checked from outside, and they must come out in the universes; they are derived and you see them beforehand"): everything derived, the comparisons' sentences and their measured signatures, is written as a gate before the record is run, blind, with its predicate, the numbers expected and the bang it may need; what one Node can show is a one-Node test, what one Node cannot show (a body's fall, a collision, the bangs, the entropy's rise, the twist's 24 and 24) is read from outside by the decoder over the record's credit lines; and in the Universe runs every derived gate must come out. A derived gate NOT SHOWN on the record is a failure to root-cause, in the engine or in the derivation, named by name and taken back to two hands, never a note left standing; the derivations are seen beforehand, and the record confirms them or the law is wrong where it says so. The record tells what it sees as it sees it (the owner's word of 2026-10-25, 11:3x UTC, through the Boss, #2187 comment 6397100411, verbatim in translation: "when there is something beautiful in the run, the decoder should write right away that this happened in the run; in effect there is a visualizer too, no? one that shows the run as an animation"): at the interval a gate first SHOWS, the decoder writes one line into the record's log naming the gate and its numbers, the record's own reading named in the table; and the page (4.0's tools/look/page.py, the torus page) reads for 5.0 the decoder's output, the board's content and the credits per interval as an animation of the torus with the gates' lines as they fire; both after the fold, by the Boss's order (6397100411). Read beside the gates 7, 10, 11 and 12 since 12:5x UTC (the hands PC1 and PC2, ALGEBRA.md, K-S4): by the letter light is the taken at every click and the taker at none, with no credit line of its own, and the sea's light has no period, so these gates are NOT SHOWN by name until a light record with a period stands; the 130-bit transient of the chiral seed is the frozen Node's volume written past the edge and not the clock's, the hands bh1 and bh2 deriving what the frozen clock stops.

### The decoder's principles (the owner's words of 2026-10-25, 07:2x, 08:0x and 08:3x UTC; ALGEBRA.md, The Node, the lines of that day)

- The decoder reads only the Node's clock and the position, with the readings the credit line carries (the point, the kind, the sense, the support, the beat, the identity, the momentum, the proper intervals), as an observer in nature does; it never reads the board's interval as an observer's quantity.
- The decoder stands at a place: a detector Node reading its own credits over its own clock and inferring the rest; two detectors share a clock only through a signal of one Link per interval.
- The decoder keeps two views apart (the owner's word of 2026-10-25, 18:0x UTC): the whole view, every click on the board, which nature does not have, the reader's table; and the one-place view, the owner's, where the decoder finds what a bound body is from the clicks alone (a Node, or a set of Nodes within one Link, credited every period with its own beat) and reads that body's clock, its proper intervals, everything of nature (the dilation, the redshift, the Links' lengthening) read from one body's clock against another's; the record's tables carry both views and the gates of general relativity are read in the second. The decoder reads in integers (18:1x UTC): every reading a quotient with its remainder, a bit length or a count, never a float.
- The whole-board log is the board's view, kept for the gates of the engine and the run back, and no observer's: a reading made on the eras or the nodes log (the volume's level U, the mean Link e^U, the horizon's Links, the Nodes turned) is a look beside the clicks and no measurement, and a gate that rests on it is marked board's below.
- All physics is in the decoder (`tools/clicks_read.py` with `tools/look/captions.py`, nothing of it in `src/`): den's circle, the species and their names, the masses, the bodies and the wells are its readings over several Nodes and several intervals; what does not show in the clicks is not in the world.
- What the lattice conserves is shown conserved in the clicks (the owner's word of 2026-10-25, 06:2x UTC GitHub's clock, through the Boss; ALGEBRA.md, The Node, **The way to a solution**): a reading the gates carry, from the credit lines alone: the charge as the senses' sum over the births (gate 17), the count as taken equals credited (gate 15), the momentum as the credits' running cosine, the energy as the beat per proper interval before and after a click, the symmetry as the clicks' images under the 48; a solution that conserves on the board and not in the clicks is not shown. On this record gates 15 and 17 carry it; the momentum, the energy and the 48 in the clicks are readings the decoder is to land by the method (tools/record_gates.py), named here as the lack.

### The record of the merged engine (2026-10-25, 13:1x UTC, the Boss's run; engine-5.0-test d752d586f)

The first long record on the merged engine, tests/worlds/world_chiral.json (world_nature.json since 2026-10-26, 04:4x UTC, the owner's word of 03:5x) at 2,400 intervals on the 5-cube (74 seconds), run by the Boss's own hand (#2187 comment 6398046061): every integer and transient within 128 bits (the state 115, the largest transient 108), 98 integers per Node constant, the run back exact 10 of 10, the charge 0 at every one of 14 births, the entropy of the clicks 1,022 bits never falling, the mass ratios of the toy's den read from the decoded points (gapped 1 over gapped 0 at 1,836,153,562 per 10^9). The fold made the symmetric ride the interval's form (the owner's sentence (1)), faithful to the toy with its ride key on (births 14, body clicks 0, the refusals 361 and 28 alike); under the ride the pump is gone (R4's finding), and with it on these seeds: body credits 0 where the morning's pumped record had 311, the era of matter NOT SHOWN, the horizon NOT REACHED (the max content 2,247 against the frozen 3,793; the collapse world of 2N = 800 on the 9-cube 1,185 at 400), no top and no bang in 2,400 (the volume's mean 1,002 at 1, 39,099 at 100, a dip to 25,690 at 200, about 10^6 by 1,400), the births all within the first 11 intervals; the morning's 130-bit transient was the pumped record's frozen Node and does not arise. So the gates of matter, of gravity's collapse and of the bangs wait on the sentences the hands W, G, V and OB named as owed, which the pump was carrying: V-D (the vacuum's weight for the counts), W-S1 (the emission), the momentum across the click (the sentence (3)), G-S4 (what sustains a body's well at the law's letter) and the merger (OB-S3); the hands bh1 and bh2 are redirected to the engine's default (whether the horizon is reached at all without the pump, then the frozen Node's step and write). The morning's SHOWN rows on the pumped toy are kept by name as that record's.

### The gates' ledger on the current head (the owner's word of 2026-10-25, 14:3x UTC by the Boss's clock, through the Boss, #2187 comment 6398664921; kept by the Scribe on every merge from the Boss's and Animate's tables)

The owner's word, verbatim in translation: "all the time make sure the things are seen in Universe24; every gate must be seen happening; if they do not happen there is a problem; everything from the seed." The Boss's standing duty from it (THE_WAY widened, BRIEF_COMMON's Addition 9): after every merge the Boss runs the record from the seed alone and reads every gate; a gate NOT SHOWN is a problem named with the item that owes it. The count is read against every row of this section, the nineteen and the widened 20 to 54 (the owner's "there are more than 19" of 14:1x UTC and his goal of 15:2x, "to bring Universe24 as close as possible to 50"): SHOWN in Universe24, N of 54, the Boss's count (6399612901); Animate, the Reader, counts N of 50, the rows 29, 30, 31 and 33 read once with 11, 10, 12 and 13 whose predicates they restate. Universe24 is the chiral seed to 2,400 from the seed alone, nothing fed; the side worlds (collapse_800 to 600, bodies to 300, hole to 900) are the reader's view beside it. ### The gates widened to everything derived, read on any bang of the same seed and at one Node where one Node can show it (2026-10-25, 11:0x UTC)

The owner's two words (the owner's word of 2026-10-25, 11:0x UTC, through the Boss, #2187 comment 6396795317, verbatim in translation: "also everything you derive and need to see ... everything that was derived and seen, that you are supposed to see as nature does, add it to the gates, because you want to see not the 19, you want to see everything that was derived, you want to see it on the board from the same seed; you need not see it in one bang, you can see it in the second, the third, the fourth, the fifth bang ... but you can also check that they are seen at one Node"; and the owner's word of 2026-10-25, 11:0x UTC, through the Boss, #2187 comment 6396810268, verbatim in translation: "everything you wrote that did not come out must enter the 19 gates; everything is to be seen there; so the order of the checks is one: you check on a Node, you check on the toy, and then it is to be seen in Universe24 too, so that in Universe24 there is no surprise that things do not happen"): every derivation that says what nature's clicks show is a gate, read by the decoder from the record of the same seed (the chiral emergence seed and the bodies' seed) on any bang of the run, a gate SHOWN on the third bang SHOWN, and each also a one-Node test where one Node can show it; every item named NOT SHOWN or a finding against nature by name in the comparisons under tools/derivations/open_items is a gate of this table with its predicate and no note, SHOWN or NOT SHOWN on the record by the decoder, nothing left out because the law lacks it. The order of the checks is one: the Node, then the toy, then Universe24; a gate that did not show on the toy is not expected on Universe24. ### The properties the engine shows, each with its test

The owner's word of 2026-10-25 (11:2x UTC, through the Boss, #2187 comment 6392645854): every property the Node showed with the families' rows keeps working under the generic Node, the Links lengthening, the reversed clock, gravity and all the rest, each with a small test showing the property exists (an existence, a sign, an order or a monotonicity; no typed course number; under 15 seconds), the list written here as the gate of every stage of the generic Node, the record's gates of the table above beside it. The inventory is the agreed derivation of two blind hands (P1, 60 properties; P2, 46 at a coarser grain; compared cold by the Boss, #2187 comment 6392787098): every property the engine shows on main has a test asserting its existence except about ten, the same ten in both lists; 17 to 19 properties lean on the families' rows and each has its re-posing for the generic Node (the same statement read from the fixed lines or from the decoder; the lean board's equality retiring into the count gate; the selector, den's circle and the body's identity moving whole to the decoder; the one sign row written by the Link's birth the open derivation). The sixty lines below are the agreed inventory in the owner's order (the Boss's post of 2026-10-25, #2187 comment 6392886380; P2's eight groups head P1's finer lines), each with its test in the tree (file::function, mapped by the Scribe from the tests' docstrings for the Boss's cold read) or the test to land; a line that leans on the families' rows carries its re-posing for the generic Node in one clause (the hands' column: the same statement read from the fixed lines or from the decoder). The ten tests named to land stand on this branch in tests/test_the_properties_hold_on_a_run.py (twelve functions on the bare 3-cube at den 1,021 under the hole seed, each an existence, a sign, an order or a monotonicity derived from the law's integers), beside the one-Node toys (tests/test_the_generic_node_toy.py and tests/test_the_generic_node_toys_close_and_constants.py, seventeen tests of the Node's acts and the owner's three constants at one Node fed through its six Links, calibrated bit for bit against the board) and the record's gates (tests/test_the_record_gates.py); the suite on the branch 123 functions in 45 files. Beside the list, the properties of nature the engine does not show stand by name in section 4 and in FURTHER_RESEARCH.md's first section.

The Links lengthening:

1. The Links lengthen as the content deepens: X rises under the light's write, e^U rises above 1, the clock and the Link's pace fall (**The volume holder's rest is the clock's one level**; **The paces compose**). tests/test_the_properties_hold_on_a_run.py::test_the_links_lengthen_on_the_running_board.
2. The lengthening is read in the clicks: the spacing of a Node's credits is longer where e^U is larger (**The click's view: the readings on the credit line**; the gate 2). tests/test_the_properties_hold_on_a_run.py::test_the_lengthening_is_read_in_the_clicks; the record's reading in tests/test_the_readers_tables.py::test_the_seven_tables_read_the_committed_record_through_the_command_line.

The reversed clock:

3. The swap at the top: a Node at a weak maximum of its volume's time line ends the interval with its levels exchanged and its remainders complemented, an involution; the floor turns the same way; the massless pair never turns (**The swap at the top**; **The floor**). tests/test_the_turn_at_the_floor.py::test_a_top_turns_every_node and ::test_the_floor_turns_as_the_top_does; tests/test_the_acts_table.py::test_the_fifth_act_the_swap_at_the_top_divides_nothing_and_is_its_own_inverse.
4. On the course a Node turns at its own top; the light's velocity flips there; the run back through the turn is exact (**The swap at the top**; **The inverse**). tests/test_the_properties_hold_on_a_run.py::test_the_clock_reverses_at_a_nodes_own_top_on_the_course.
5. Ten thousand phase acts forward and back return the seed bit for bit (**The sign holder rotates the two-part record**). tests/test_the_phase_line.py::test_the_reversal_is_bit_exact_over_ten_thousand_acts.

Gravity:

6. Gravity: a record's form sources the volume at its Node and nowhere beyond one Link; X rises there and the clock slows there (**The volume holder's rest is the clock's one level**; **Only local**). tests/test_the_properties_hold_on_a_run.py::test_gravitys_sign_on_two_boards.
7. The dilation of the proper clock: the deeper Node counts fewer proper intervals (**The paces compose**; the gate 10). tests/test_the_properties_hold_on_a_run.py::test_the_dilations_sign_by_hand.
8. The horizon: a Link at the wall Gamma is read at the wall, its two ends decoupled, re-coupled when the tension falls; the run back exact through it (**The horizon**). tests/test_the_properties_hold_on_a_run.py::test_the_horizon_is_forced_at_the_wall.
9. The frozen clock: at the frozen content the Link's pace is 0, the Node holds on that axis, is not read and births nothing (**The paces compose**, the frozen content). tests/test_the_properties_hold_on_a_run.py::test_the_frozen_clock_holds_and_births_nothing.
10. The returns of the volume: the uniform board's contraction is its expansion run back, the seed met again; the structured board's returns shallow (**The swap at the top**; FURTHER_RESEARCH.md, No return on a board whose Nodes top apart). tests/test_the_properties_hold_on_a_run.py::test_the_uniform_sea_turns_at_every_node_at_once_and_runs_its_rise_back.
11. The pump: the massless seed's share deepens with the content, the light not spent, a finding by name (**The board reads itself**, the exact take; FURTHER_RESEARCH.md, The lay lock). tests/test_the_hole_stops_the_drift.py::test_the_5_cube_keeps_its_light_above_zero_and_makes_nothing_under_the_takes (the light above zero and nothing made under the takes); leans on the families' rows: re-posed as the massless line's share read from the fixed lines.

The Node's acts:

12. Rule3 is one carried division, exact forward and back (**The flux form of Rule3**; **The inverse**). tests/test_rule3.py::test_the_one_rule_steps_forward_and_back_exactly_on_integers_and_int64_arrays.
13. The held write is one carried division, exact forward and back, every remainder born at half the wall (**The primitives**, the held write). tests/test_the_write_is_one_act.py::test_the_held_write_is_one_carried_division_forward_and_back and ::test_the_one_write_per_line_on_the_bare_torus_is_the_act_by_hand.
14. The coefficients at the composed paces: the clock once, the Link twice; the paces' group law (**The paces compose**). tests/test_rule3.py::test_the_composed_paces_are_the_laws_two_functions_of_the_content_and_their_factors and ::test_the_coefficients_are_the_laws_line_and_the_isotropic_ones_at_zero_axis_contents.
15. The guard refuses a pace beyond the edge by name; a hill enters as it is (**The guard**). tests/test_the_features.py::test_a_hill_enters_as_it_is_and_the_guard_refuses_a_pace_beyond_the_edge_by_name.
16. The conserved form at fixed reads, to the remainders; the lattice momentum; every conserved quantity a bound (**The flux form of Rule3**; **The lattice momentum**). tests/test_the_flux_form.py::test_the_linear_sum_of_light_is_constant_on_the_bare_torus_until_the_births and ::test_the_rate_weighted_linear_sum_of_light_is_constant_at_a_fixed_content; tests/test_the_lattice_momentum.py::test_the_momentum_is_conserved_exactly_in_the_mirror_and_to_the_floor_on_the_integers.
17. The tension is Rule3's own conservation of the current; the stress is the Links' (**The tension**; **The stress is the Links'**). tests/test_the_node.py::test_the_tension_is_rule3s_own_conservation_of_the_current.
18. One Node's acts equal Rule3 by hand; the engine's step is the law's first form (**The one Node**; **The flux form of Rule3**). tests/test_the_node.py::test_one_nodes_acts_are_rule3_called_by_hand; tests/test_step_oracle.py::test_the_engines_step_is_the_laws_first_form_on_the_bare_torus.
19. The acts table: every division of src listed with its kind; the swap divides nothing (**The engine's derivation ledger**; the gates). tests/test_the_acts_table.py::test_the_acts_table_lists_every_division_of_src_with_its_kind and ::test_the_fifth_act_the_swap_at_the_top_divides_nothing_and_is_its_own_inverse.

## 5. The commands

The record's run, the decoder, the two pages and the run back, from a checkout's root with the package installed (Python 3.14 or later; README.md, Run it):

```
PYTHONPATH=src nice -n 10 python tools/eras.py examples/events/universe24/universe24.json --every 100 --span 50 --out examples/events/universe24/universe24.eras.jsonl --clicks examples/events/universe24/universe24.clicks.jsonl --nodes examples/events/universe24/universe24.nodes.jsonl
PYTHONPATH=src python tools/clicks_read.py examples/events/universe24/universe24.clicks.jsonl --shape 5 5 5 --eras examples/events/universe24/universe24.eras.jsonl --nodes examples/events/universe24/universe24.nodes.jsonl --out examples/events/universe24/readings_clicks.json
PYTHONPATH=src python tools/look/eras_page.py examples/events/universe24/universe24.eras.jsonl --clicks examples/events/universe24/universe24.clicks.jsonl --nodes examples/events/universe24/universe24.nodes.jsonl --notes examples/events/universe24/notes.md --out examples/events/universe24/universe24.html
PYTHONPATH=src python tools/look/torus_page.py examples/events/universe24/universe24.nodes.jsonl --eras examples/events/universe24/universe24.eras.jsonl --clicks examples/events/universe24/universe24.clicks.jsonl --world examples/events/universe24/universe24.json --notes examples/events/universe24/notes.md --out examples/events/universe24/universe24.torus.html
PYTHONPATH=src python tools/back_in_time.py examples/events/universe24/universe24.json --from K --intervals N
PYTHONPATH=src python tools/node_integers.py examples/events/universe24/universe24.json --intervals 13 --bits 128
```

The clicks log is the record; the eras and the nodes logs are looks beside it. The tests that touch the record: tests/test_universe24_runs_its_cycle.py (the bare file loads; the small bare torus is seeded with the hole, bangs and births its pairs), tests/test_the_readers_tables.py (the decoder's tables by hand and the smoke check of the thirteen tables on the committed record), tests/test_a_click_does_what_it_should.py (the owner's standing test of the click). The check before every landing: `PYTHONPATH=src python tools/check.py --base main`.
